    @pytest.mark.parametrize("reverse", ORDERS)

    def test_malformed_duplicate_cannot_hide_another_identity_conflict(
        fake_machine, route, reverse, omission, field, value,
    ):
        first = _target()
        second = copy.deepcopy(first)
        second[field] = value
        malformed_field, malformed_value = MALFORMED[omission]
        assert malformed_field != field
        second[malformed_field] = malformed_value
        pair = [first, second]
        if reverse:
            pair.reverse()
>       _assert_duplicate_refused(fake_machine, {"blockdevices": [_boot(), *pair]}, route)

        fake_machine, route, reverse, omission, field, value,
    ):
        first = _target()
        second = copy.deepcopy(first)
        second[field] = value
        malformed_field, malformed_value = MALFORMED[omission]
        assert malformed_field != field
        second[malformed_field] = malformed_value
        pair = [first, second]
        if reverse:
            pair.reverse()
>       _assert_duplicate_refused(fake_machine, {"blockdevices": [_boot(), *pair]}, route)

tests/test_discovery_observation_fallback_pass16.py:154: AssertionError
_ test_cmdline_fallback_considers_every_physical_medium[disk-rom-128000000000-device-False-False-injected] _

fake_machine = <function fake_machine.<locals>.scan at 0x749044a598a0>
route = 'injected', reverse = False, mounted = False, source_form = 'device'
competitor_size = 128000000000, competitor_kind = 'rom', hit_kind = 'disk'

    @pytest.mark.parametrize("route", PUBLIC_ROUTES)
    @pytest.mark.parametrize("reverse", ORDERS)
    @pytest.mark.parametrize("mounted", (False, True))
    @pytest.mark.parametrize("source_form", ("device", "label", "uuid"))
    @pytest.mark.parametrize("competitor_size", (0, 128_000_000_000))
    @pytest.mark.parametrize("competitor_kind", ("disk", "rom"))
    @pytest.mark.parametrize("hit_kind", ("disk", "rom"))
    def test_cmdline_fallback_considers_every_physical_medium(
        fake_machine, route, reverse, mounted, source_form, competitor_size,
        competitor_kind, hit_kind,
    ):
        hit_name = "sdz" if hit_kind == "disk" else "sr0"
        other_name = "sda" if competitor_kind == "disk" else "sr1"
        hit = _disk(
            hit_name, type=hit_kind, tran="usb", ro=hit_kind == "rom",
            size=16_000_000_000, label="FAKE-LIVE", uuid="fake-live-uuid",
        )
        other = _disk(other_name, type=competitor_kind, size=competitor_size,
                      ro=competitor_kind == "rom")
        source = {
            "device": hit["path"], "label": "LABEL=FAKE-LIVE", "uuid": "UUID=fake-live-uuid",
        }[source_form]
        rows = [hit, other]
        if reverse:
            rows.reverse()
        mounts = [hit["path"]] if mounted else []
        cmdline = "boot=live live-media=" + source
        expected = mounted or competitor_size == 0
        # Contrast the resolver's direct contract with both public parsing routes.
        identified = discovery.identify_boot_path(
            rows, mount_sources=mounts, cmdline=cmdline, require_device_link=False,
        )
>       assert identified == (hit["path"] if expected else None)
E       AssertionError: assert '/dev/sdz' == None

    @pytest.mark.parametrize("competitor_kind", ("disk", "rom"))
    @pytest.mark.parametrize("hit_kind", ("disk", "rom"))
    def test_cmdline_fallback_considers_every_physical_medium(
        fake_machine, route, reverse, mounted, source_form, competitor_size,
        competitor_kind, hit_kind,
    ):
        hit_name = "sdz" if hit_kind == "disk" else "sr0"
        other_name = "sda" if competitor_kind == "disk" else "sr1"
        hit = _disk(
            hit_name, type=hit_kind, tran="usb", ro=hit_kind == "rom",
            size=16_000_000_000, label="FAKE-LIVE", uuid="fake-live-uuid",
        )
        other = _disk(other_name, type=competitor_kind, size=competitor_size,
                      ro=competitor_kind == "rom")
        source = {
            "device": hit["path"], "label": "LABEL=FAKE-LIVE", "uuid": "UUID=fake-live-uuid",
        }[source_form]
        rows = [hit, other]
        if reverse:
            rows.reverse()
        mounts = [hit["path"]] if mounted else []
        cmdline = "boot=live live-media=" + source
        expected = mounted or competitor_size == 0
        # Contrast the resolver's direct contract with both public parsing routes.
        identified = discovery.identify_boot_path(
            rows, mount_sources=mounts, cmdline=cmdline, require_device_link=False,
        )
>       assert identified == (hit["path"] if expected else None)
E       AssertionError: assert '/dev/sdz' == None

    @pytest.mark.parametrize("route", PUBLIC_ROUTES)
    @pytest.mark.parametrize("reverse", ORDERS)
    @pytest.mark.parametrize("mounted", (False, True))
    @pytest.mark.parametrize("source_form", ("device", "label", "uuid"))
    @pytest.mark.parametrize("competitor_size", (0, 128_000_000_000))
    @pytest.mark.parametrize("competitor_kind", ("disk", "rom"))
    @pytest.mark.parametrize("hit_kind", ("disk", "rom"))
    def test_cmdline_fallback_considers_every_physical_medium(
        fake_machine, route, reverse, mounted, source_form, competitor_size,
        competitor_kind, hit_kind,
    ):
        hit_name = "sdz" if hit_kind == "disk" else "sr0"
        other_name = "sda" if competitor_kind == "disk" else "sr1"
        hit = _disk(
            hit_name, type=hit_kind, tran="usb", ro=hit_kind == "rom",
            size=16_000_000_000, label="FAKE-LIVE", uuid="fake-live-uuid",
        )
        other = _disk(other_name, type=competitor_kind, size=competitor_size,
                      ro=competitor_kind == "rom")
        source = {
            "device": hit["path"], "label": "LABEL=FAKE-LIVE", "uuid": "UUID=fake-live-uuid",
        }[source_form]
        rows = [hit, other]
        if reverse:
            rows.reverse()
        mounts = [hit["path"]] if mounted else []
        cmdline = "boot=live live-media=" + source
        expected = mounted or competitor_size == 0
        # Contrast the resolver's direct contract with both public parsing routes.
        identified = discovery.identify_boot_path(
            rows, mount_sources=mounts, cmdline=cmdline, require_device_link=False,
        )
>       assert identified == (hit["path"] if expected else None)
E       AssertionError: assert '/dev/sr0' == None

fake_machine = <function fake_machine.<locals>.scan at 0x749044a48cc0>

    @pytest.mark.parametrize("route", PUBLIC_ROUTES)
    @pytest.mark.parametrize("reverse", ORDERS)
    @pytest.mark.parametrize("mounted", (False, True))
    @pytest.mark.parametrize("source_form", ("device", "label", "uuid"))
    @pytest.mark.parametrize("competitor_size", (0, 128_000_000_000))
    @pytest.mark.parametrize("competitor_kind", ("disk", "rom"))
    @pytest.mark.parametrize("hit_kind", ("disk", "rom"))
    def test_cmdline_fallback_considers_every_physical_medium(
        fake_machine, route, reverse, mounted, source_form, competitor_size,
        competitor_kind, hit_kind,
    ):
        hit_name = "sdz" if hit_kind == "disk" else "sr0"
        other_name = "sda" if competitor_kind == "disk" else "sr1"
        hit = _disk(
            hit_name, type=hit_kind, tran="usb", ro=hit_kind == "rom",
            size=16_000_000_000, label="FAKE-LIVE", uuid="fake-live-uuid",
        )
        other = _disk(other_name, type=competitor_kind, size=competitor_size,
                      ro=competitor_kind == "rom")
        source = {
            "device": hit["path"], "label": "LABEL=FAKE-LIVE", "uuid": "UUID=fake-live-uuid",
        }[source_form]
        rows = [hit, other]
        if reverse:
            rows.reverse()
        mounts = [hit["path"]] if mounted else []
        cmdline = "boot=live live-media=" + source
        expected = mounted or competitor_size == 0
        # Contrast the resolver's direct contract with both public parsing routes.
        identified = discovery.identify_boot_path(
            rows, mount_sources=mounts, cmdline=cmdline, require_device_link=False,
        )
>       assert identified == (hit["path"] if expected else None)

tests/test_discovery_observation_fallback_pass16.py:282: AssertionError
_ test_native_escape_closes_modal_once_and_waits_for_a_fresh_press[False-owner] _

ui = <function ui.<locals>.build at 0x749044a4b060>
native = <class 'test_accessible_focus_input_pass11.native.<locals>.Input'>
screen = <Screen.OWNER: 'owner'>, rerender = False

    @pytest.mark.parametrize("screen", [Screen.OWNER, Screen.METHOD, Screen.LAST_CHANCE, Screen.WORKING])
    @pytest.mark.parametrize("rerender", [False, True])
    def test_native_escape_closes_modal_once_and_waits_for_a_fresh_press(ui, native, screen, rerender):  # noqa: F811
        app, clock = ui(screen)
        controller = _open(app)
        if rerender:
            app.render()
            _drain()
            assert app._sound_dialog is controller and controller.dialog is not None
        before = _authority(app.w)
        observed = []
        releases = []
    
        def observe(_widget, event):
            if event.type == Gdk.EventType.KEY_PRESS and event.keyval == Gdk.KEY_Escape:
                observed.append(event.time)
            if event.type == Gdk.EventType.KEY_RELEASE and event.keyval == Gdk.KEY_Escape:
                releases.append(event.time)
            return False
    
        # GtkWidget::event precedes its key-specific handler, including a handler
        # that returns True to consume repeats. This only observes native events.
        controller.dialog.connect("event", observe)
        app.window.connect("event", observe)
        native.key(Gdk.KEY_Escape, True)
        _wait(lambda: controller.dialog is None)
        _drain(0.85)
        assert len(observed) >= 2, "The private X server did not deliver native hold-repeat"
>       assert app.w.screen is screen
E       AssertionError: assert <Screen.KEYBOARD: 'keyboard'> is <Screen.OWNER: 'owner'>
E        +  where <Screen.KEYBOARD: 'keyboard'> = <beamo_wipe.wizard.Wizard object at 0x749055a9a030>.screen
E        +    where <beamo_wipe.wizard.Wizard object at 0x749055a9a030> = <beamo_wipe.ui.accessible_wizard.AccessibleWizard object at 0x749055a763c0>.w

    def test_native_escape_closes_modal_once_and_waits_for_a_fresh_press(ui, native, screen, rerender):  # noqa: F811
        app, clock = ui(screen)
        controller = _open(app)
        if rerender:
            app.render()
            _drain()
            assert app._sound_dialog is controller and controller.dialog is not None
        before = _authority(app.w)
        observed = []
        releases = []
    
        def observe(_widget, event):
            if event.type == Gdk.EventType.KEY_PRESS and event.keyval == Gdk.KEY_Escape:
                observed.append(event.time)
            if event.type == Gdk.EventType.KEY_RELEASE and event.keyval == Gdk.KEY_Escape:
                releases.append(event.time)
            return False
    
        # GtkWidget::event precedes its key-specific handler, including a handler
        # that returns True to consume repeats. This only observes native events.
        controller.dialog.connect("event", observe)
        app.window.connect("event", observe)
        native.key(Gdk.KEY_Escape, True)
        _wait(lambda: controller.dialog is None)
        _drain(0.85)
        assert len(observed) >= 2, "The private X server did not deliver native hold-repeat"

_ test_obsolete_confirmation_callbacks_do_not_outlive_their_owner[redraw-idle-standard] _

ui = <function ui.<locals>.build at 0x7490449c1da0>
monkeypatch = <_pytest.monkeypatch.MonkeyPatch object at 0x749055d47500>
text_size = 'standard', pending = 'idle', lifecycle = 'redraw'

    @pytest.mark.parametrize("text_size", ["standard", "large", "extra"])
    @pytest.mark.parametrize("pending", ["idle", "retry"])
    @pytest.mark.parametrize("lifecycle", ["redraw", "navigate", "destroy"])
    def test_obsolete_confirmation_callbacks_do_not_outlive_their_owner(
        ui, monkeypatch, text_size, pending, lifecycle
    ):
        app, _clock, errors = ui(text_size)
        # Match run()'s mapped native input claim before creating the Entry whose
        # pending work this case retires. Never replace child focus after retirement.
        app.root.geometry("800x600+0+0")
        app.root.update()
        app.root.focus_force()
        app._draw()
        old_entry = _entry(app)
        before = _authority(app.w)
        attempts = []
        if pending == "retry":
            original = app._ensure_confirm_focus
    
            def observe(entry, *args, **kwargs):
                attempts.append((entry, app.root.focus_get() is entry))
                return original(entry, *args, **kwargs)
    
            monkeypatch.setattr(app, "_ensure_confirm_focus", observe)
            app.root.withdraw()
            app.root.update_idletasks()
            assert any(entry is old_entry and not focused for entry, focused in attempts)
            assert not errors
    
        if lifecycle != "destroy":
            app.root.deiconify()
            # Claim native input before the renderer establishes its child focus.
            # A later root.focus_force() would replace that correct child focus.
            app.root.focus_force()
        if lifecycle == "navigate":
            app.w.back()
            before = _authority(app.w)
            assert app.w.screen is Screen.PICK
            app._draw()
        elif lifecycle == "redraw":
            app._draw()
        else:
            app._teardown()
    
        assert not app.root.tk.call("info", "commands", str(old_entry))
        if lifecycle == "destroy":
            _pump(app.root)
        else:
            app.root.update()
            _pump(app.root)
            focus = app.root.focus_get()
            assert focus is not old_entry
            if lifecycle == "redraw":
                current = _entry(app)
                assert focus is current
                assert current.get() == app.w.confirm_input
            else:
                assert app.w.screen is Screen.PICK
                assert focus is app._primary
>       assert not errors
E       assert not [('Tcl bgerror', 'invalid command name "128163261133120<lambda>"'), ('Tcl bgerror', 'invalid command name "128162984488192<lambda>"'), ('Tcl bgerror', 'invalid command name "128162983037376<lambda>"')]

_ test_obsolete_confirmation_callbacks_do_not_outlive_their_owner[navigate-idle-standard] _

ui = <function ui.<locals>.build at 0x749055a9e020>
monkeypatch = <_pytest.monkeypatch.MonkeyPatch object at 0x749055605b80>
text_size = 'standard', pending = 'idle', lifecycle = 'navigate'

    @pytest.mark.parametrize("text_size", ["standard", "large", "extra"])
    @pytest.mark.parametrize("pending", ["idle", "retry"])
    @pytest.mark.parametrize("lifecycle", ["redraw", "navigate", "destroy"])
    def test_obsolete_confirmation_callbacks_do_not_outlive_their_owner(
        ui, monkeypatch, text_size, pending, lifecycle
    ):
        app, _clock, errors = ui(text_size)
        # Match run()'s mapped native input claim before creating the Entry whose
        # pending work this case retires. Never replace child focus after retirement.
        app.root.geometry("800x600+0+0")
        app.root.update()
        app.root.focus_force()
        app._draw()
        old_entry = _entry(app)
        before = _authority(app.w)
        attempts = []
        if pending == "retry":
            original = app._ensure_confirm_focus
    
            def observe(entry, *args, **kwargs):
                attempts.append((entry, app.root.focus_get() is entry))
                return original(entry, *args, **kwargs)
    
            monkeypatch.setattr(app, "_ensure_confirm_focus", observe)
            app.root.withdraw()
            app.root.update_idletasks()
            assert any(entry is old_entry and not focused for entry, focused in attempts)
            assert not errors
    
        if lifecycle != "destroy":
            app.root.deiconify()
            # Claim native input before the renderer establishes its child focus.
            # A later root.focus_force() would replace that correct child focus.
            app.root.focus_force()
        if lifecycle == "navigate":
            app.w.back()
            before = _authority(app.w)
            assert app.w.screen is Screen.PICK
            app._draw()
        elif lifecycle == "redraw":
            app._draw()
        else:
            app._teardown()
    
        assert not app.root.tk.call("info", "commands", str(old_entry))
        if lifecycle == "destroy":
            _pump(app.root)
        else:
            app.root.update()
            _pump(app.root)
            focus = app.root.focus_get()
            assert focus is not old_entry
            if lifecycle == "redraw":
                current = _entry(app)
                assert focus is current
                assert current.get() == app.w.confirm_input
            else:
                assert app.w.screen is Screen.PICK
                assert focus is app._primary

tests/test_confirm_focus_callback_lifetime_pass16.py:163: AssertionError
_ test_obsolete_confirmation_callbacks_do_not_outlive_their_owner[destroy-idle-extra] _

ui = <function ui.<locals>.build at 0x749044d75a80>
monkeypatch = <_pytest.monkeypatch.MonkeyPatch object at 0x749044bacec0>
text_size = 'extra', pending = 'idle', lifecycle = 'destroy'

    @pytest.mark.parametrize("text_size", ["standard", "large", "extra"])
    @pytest.mark.parametrize("pending", ["idle", "retry"])
    @pytest.mark.parametrize("lifecycle", ["redraw", "navigate", "destroy"])
    def test_obsolete_confirmation_callbacks_do_not_outlive_their_owner(
        ui, monkeypatch, text_size, pending, lifecycle
    ):
        app, _clock, errors = ui(text_size)
        # Match run()'s mapped native input claim before creating the Entry whose
        # pending work this case retires. Never replace child focus after retirement.
        app.root.geometry("800x600+0+0")
        app.root.update()
        app.root.focus_force()
        app._draw()
        old_entry = _entry(app)
        before = _authority(app.w)
        attempts = []
        if pending == "retry":
            original = app._ensure_confirm_focus
    
            def observe(entry, *args, **kwargs):
                attempts.append((entry, app.root.focus_get() is entry))
                return original(entry, *args, **kwargs)
    
            monkeypatch.setattr(app, "_ensure_confirm_focus", observe)
            app.root.withdraw()
            app.root.update_idletasks()
            assert any(entry is old_entry and not focused for entry, focused in attempts)
            assert not errors
    
        if lifecycle != "destroy":
            app.root.deiconify()
            # Claim native input before the renderer establishes its child focus.
            # A later root.focus_force() would replace that correct child focus.
            app.root.focus_force()
        if lifecycle == "navigate":
            app.w.back()
            before = _authority(app.w)
            assert app.w.screen is Screen.PICK
            app._draw()
        elif lifecycle == "redraw":
            app._draw()
        else:
            app._teardown()
    
        assert not app.root.tk.call("info", "commands", str(old_entry))
        if lifecycle == "destroy":
            _pump(app.root)
        else:
            app.root.update()
            _pump(app.root)
            focus = app.root.focus_get()
            assert focus is not old_entry
            if lifecycle == "redraw":
                current = _entry(app)
                assert focus is current
                assert current.get() == app.w.confirm_input
            else:
                assert app.w.screen is Screen.PICK
                assert focus is app._primary
>       assert not errors
E       assert not [('Tcl bgerror', 'invalid command name "128162977395008<lambda>"'), ('Tcl bgerror', 'invalid command name "128163261542528<lambda>"'), ('Tcl bgerror', 'invalid command name "128162977686336<lambda>"')]

ui = <function ui.<locals>.build at 0x749044d75260>

    def test_redraw_retires_child_work_but_preserves_container_and_peer_work(ui):
        app, _clock, errors = ui("standard")
        peer = tk.Tk()
        peer.withdraw()
        peer_errors = []
        peer.report_callback_exception = lambda kind, error, _trace: peer_errors.append((kind.__name__, str(error)))
        peer.createcommand("pass16_peer_bgerror", lambda message: peer_errors.append(("Tcl bgerror", message)))
        peer.tk.eval("proc bgerror {message} {pass16_peer_bgerror $message}")
        retired, live_container, other_interpreter = [], [], []
        before = _authority(app.w)
        try:
            _entry(app).after(0, lambda: retired.append("retired child"))
            app._body.after(0, lambda: live_container.append("live container"))
            peer.after(0, lambda: other_interpreter.append("peer"))
            app._draw()
            _pump(app.root)
            assert retired == []
            assert live_container == ["live container"]
            assert other_interpreter == ["peer"]
>           assert not errors and not peer_errors
E           assert (not [('Tcl bgerror', 'invalid command name "128162977682624<lambda>"'), ('Tcl bgerror', 'invalid command name "128162977683072<lambda>"')])
